# Classic → React parity evidence ledger

Audit baseline: checkpoint `3e2f95b5`, 2026-09-21. Phase 4 is **open**.

A classification is a product requirement/disposition, not a passing result. `Required` and `Redesigned equivalent` rows have executable acceptance scenarios below; existing code or a matching command string is only implementation evidence. Unless a named test/run is recorded, acceptance is **unverified**. `Missing` and `Present but behaviorally degraded` remain required repair work. No feature is silently retired.

Sources below are relative to `static/js/`; React owners are under `ui/src/`. All runtime acceptance uses an isolated profile and port. The live daemon must not be deployed, stopped or restarted.

## Behavior matrix

Each literal classic transport command is inventoried independently. Dynamic command families and native operations are covered by the behavior rows and the source inventory below. A React occurrence does not establish a discoverable control, correct payload, response handling or parity. These are required verification rows unless a specific retirement decision says otherwise.

## Validation record

- Baseline: `make ui-check` passed (11 files, 102 tests, lint/typecheck/build and build scan).
- Baseline: `make lint-docs-contract` passed (71 Markdown files); `git diff --check` passed.
- Browser, packaged Tauri, full backend suite and individual rows above have not yet been validated in this audit.
- Phase 4 and classic retirement remain blocked while required gaps or unverified product decisions remain.

## Settings field comparison

Each row is a Required field contract, implemented by `StructuredSettings.tsx` or its explicit Settings panel control. Defaults are hydrated from the daemon; descriptions retain dataclass inheritance/unit notes. Opening the form waits for all settings responses. An unchanged inherited relay value is not promoted to an explicit override. Choices/min/max originate from the classic form and are server-validated on save. Daemon provider/model/reasoning choices and per-agent reset/override intent now have focused coverage. Daemon-backed section/field resets and sparse saves now have named coverage below. The original 163-row inventory includes runtime records and the classic `provider` alias; those are identified below rather than presented as editable defaults. Nested GitHub number/maps omitted by the original inventory are included. Exhaustive non-default boundary/enum and runtime-effect checks remain open; default write/reload and typed-control coverage do not certify those effects.

Acceptance for **each field**: hydrate its server value, change it using the listed control, save, reopen and compare; exercise its boundary/enum validation and verify unrelated incoming data preserves draft/focus. Generic control and coordinated-save tests cover shared mechanics, not every field's backend effect.

| Field | Scope | Control / choices | Description / constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `default_command` | Global | Text | empty = use config.DEFAULT_COMMAND (env var fallback) |
| `filter_by_window` | Group, Global | Enabled/Disabled | global default for window filtering |
| `focus_new_tabs` | Global | Enabled/Disabled | switch focus to newly created tabs |
| `focus_on_click` | Global | Enabled/Disabled | single click also focuses the terminal |
| `max_pipeline_depth` | Global | Number | 0 = unlimited; min=0 |
| `max_event_log` | Global | Number | max persisted panel events; min=50, max=10000 |
| `github_repo` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `github_project_owner` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `github_project_status_field` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `board_sync_enabled` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Server value/default |
| `provider` | Classic alias | Uses `board_sync_provider` | One group sync-provider control; not a CLI provider setting. |
| `default_agent_template` | Group | Scoped role/template selector | None; Project/User display names; unavailable saved value retained |
| `agent_provider` | Group | Text | adapter name ("claude-code", "codex", etc.) — empty = use default |
| `agent_boot_command` | Group | Text | override default boot command (e.g. "codex") |
| `agent_model` | Group | Text | default model override when provider supports it |
| `agent_reasoning_effort` | Group | Text | default reasoning-effort override |
| `agent_fast_mode` | Group | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `worker_provider` | Group | Text | adapter override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_boot_command` | Group | Text | boot command override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_model` | Group | Text | model override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_reasoning_effort` | Group | Text | reasoning override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_fast_mode` | Group | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `agent_directory` | Group | Text | Server value/default |
| `agent_shell` | Group | Select: inherit, zsh, bash, fish | Server value/default |
| `engineer_merge_mode` | Group | Select: pr, direct, engineer-choice | pr \| direct \| engineer-choice |
| `worktree_merge_cleanup` | Group | Select: keep, auto_sweep, close, remove, close_remove | keep \| close \| remove \| close_remove \| auto_sweep |

## Repair evidence and remaining release gates

The original dirty stack was validated and checkpointed as `3e2f95b5` before feature work. This repair pass adds the following evidence; required rows not named here remain unverified.

| Behavior rows | Concrete evidence | Limits |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P-001–P-007 | `features/control/parity.test.tsx`: bounded tail, regex/level, target switch/abort, follow pause, retention and errors. Playwright checks real bounded endpoint plus controlled filtering; Logs screenshot inspected. | Native reveal and actual supervisor log production require desktop burn-in. |
| P-008–P-011 | Component and browser tests: cross-group threads, progressive history/context, stable selected thread/disclosure under updates. | Client renders the retained snapshot only; older unavailable history is explicitly labelled. |
| P-014–P-017 | Component cycle-layout/keyboard/result-isolation tests; live discovery of saved cyclic project actions, zoom/fit and action-editor handoff. Pipeline screenshot inspected. Preview survives unrelated responses. | Large-graph and pointer-pan soak remain release QA. |
| P-018–P-024, P-034–P-035 | Agent model saved-order regression; App tests for terminal `reorder_child`/`reparent_terminal` plus resync, arbitrary group order, selected/hidden lanes. | Full hierarchy move/reload acceptance remains a live QA gate. Group move preserves ownership and moves attached terminals, not owned workers/engineers. |
| P-048, P-061, P-063–P-064 | Agents browser fixture: three-level hierarchy, Live/Activity round trip, keyboard separators, preserved composer draft, collapse. | Separate isolated real-PTY browser QA typed a marker through React xterm into `/bin/cat` and observed the returned output. Detached PTY handoff under live output remains unverified. |
| P-066–P-072, P-074–P-077 | Paging projection deduplicates and retains live events; component ask reply-target/draft/copy/filter test; supervisor sort/detail/persistence/unmount; health scope/window/history charts with sample tables. | Attention model/component and browser evidence is extended by P-116–P-118; detailed frontend render attribution remains open. Native supervisor restart/kill intentionally not exercised. |
| P-080, P-082 | Live Planning create/edit/link/reopen test; component link/unlink delta keeps draft; compact mutation replies rehydrate relationships. | Area component/projection and live browser lifecycle/link/note evidence is extended by P-121–P-122; other Planning lifecycles remain separately unverified. |
| P-087–P-094 | Typed controls replace raw settings JSON; component bool/number/list/map edits, acknowledged coordinated save and partial-failure draft retention. | Server normalizers still own validation; each field contract above needs round-trip acceptance. |
| P-106, P-108, P-110 | Browser Tauri adapter fixture checks detach, hidden source hierarchy, reattach commands; host unit tests remain in UI gate. Main detached Agents disables its PTY while retaining DM draft. | Separate macOS native QA verified Agents detach/close/reopen, Retina bounds, Reload and Show Logs. Other platforms, crash/sleep recovery and live PTY ownership transitions remain unverified. |
| P-116–P-118 | `features/attention/attention.test.tsx`: reply precedence, inactive targets, group scope, correlated acknowledgement, failed delivery and draft/focus/caret retention; exact diff/hash/base, user-only approval, dedupe and stale failure. Browser fixture exercises failed reply and reviewed rejection. | Real-daemon coverage is extended by P-130–P-133; the earlier fixture remains a deterministic failure-path test. Commercial provider semantic acceptance and crash-window exactly-once delivery are not certified. |
| P-130–P-133 | Backend tests cover concurrent/replayed asks, immutable retry, recorded delivery recovery, reviewed hash/base guards and legacy CLI compatibility. Real-daemon Playwright checks a generic Python PTY receiver, HTTP transport failure/retry and competing client, closed replay, compact context refresh, late reconnect snapshots, and SQLite-backed approve/stale/reload/reject. Both drafts retain focus/caret on reconnect. | No commercial provider invocation, process-crash delivery guarantee, or native window test in this checkpoint. Existing native/Planning gates remain open. |
| P-119–P-120 | Provider-choice model tests and App settings tests cover metadata discovery, model-specific reasoning, custom choices, sparse save/reset, explicit empty instructions, unrelated updates and failed save retention. | Section/field resets are covered by P-125–P-128. Exhaustive non-default boundary/enum checks remain open. |
| P-121–P-122 | `features/planning/area.test.tsx` covers lifecycle enums, grouped links/composite removal, note updates/archive and draft retention. Live browser creates/edits/reopens an Area, links an initiative and Area, edits/archives a targeted note, and unlinks. Area screenshot inspected. | Every note type/target/relation combination and remaining Planning lifecycle flows still need acceptance. |
| P-123–P-124 | Host/App tests cover retained bounds, stale close labels, startup snapshot ordering and missing native windows. Rust tests cover physical/legacy Retina sizing; backend test persists the physical marker. Native macOS resize/close/reopen preserved 2000×1360 physical inner pixels at x=828/y=246 with a new native label; Reload recovered stale ownership. | Fresh process restart after the final snapshot-order fix, cross-monitor moves, crash/sleep recovery and other platforms remain open. |
| P-125–P-128 | `settings.test.tsx`, App settings tests, and `test_react_settings_contract.py`: staged resets, sparse edits, draft retention under updates/errors, runtime field exclusion, typed schema/default coverage and Engineer command persistence. Browser settings scenario verifies typed edits, unrelated external change, staged reset and reopen against an isolated daemon. | 166 scope-field default contracts include 19 Architect duplicates in Group storage. A separate 181-choice audit persisted/reloaded every offered fixed dropdown and boolean choice. Numeric/text boundaries and live runtime effects remain open. |
| P-129 | `test_react_settings_contract.py` verifies Engineer and per-agent single/bulk/restart values plus missing-column migration. The final 181-choice SQLite audit passed after removing the repeated read-time heuristic. | Runtime heartbeat scheduling and external provider behavior are separate from value persistence. |

**Still open:** command/modal inventory rows without named execution evidence; settings numeric/text boundary and runtime-effect acceptance; complete attention live delivery/approval evidence; remaining Area/Planning targets and lifecycle combinations; remaining lazy-panel reconnect coverage; complete native lifecycle tests. The classic fallback and Phase 4 remain open. Canvas coordinates and arbitrary docked/floating compositions are explicitly retired under D-077; no other Missing row is waived.

## Pointer/layout operation inventory

| Operation | Classification | Acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Root-agent position (`commands.js` ordering handlers) | Redesigned equivalent | Move/Reorder places a root before any eligible root and reload preserves it. |
| Owned-agent sibling position | Redesigned equivalent | Reorder inside its ownership tier without changing owner IDs. |
| Terminal sibling position | Redesigned equivalent | Dedicated reorder_child persists before/after placement and resync shows it. |
| Terminal attach/detach | Redesigned equivalent | reparent_terminal preserves terminal identity, parent/group rules and selection. |
| Group-tab ordering | Required | Pointer drop and keyboard Move group insert at beginning/middle/end, surviving reload. |
| Task card/subtree lane drop | Required | Move subtree within/across lanes; reject invalid derived-task placement and retain independent scroll. |
| Board lane reorder | Required | Reorder eligible lanes without orphaning tasks or changing hidden/selected preferences. |
| Task multi-selection bulk move | Required | Selected task set and destination survive filter/delta; only selected tasks move. |
| Artifact/file drop | Required | Drop files onto correct task/composer, inspect previews and remove before send. |
| Sidebar separator | Redesigned equivalent | Pointer and keyboard resize clamps width and persists it. |
| Terminal/DM separator | Redesigned equivalent | Pointer and keyboard resize clamps height and retains draft, caret and scroll. |
| Composer separator | Redesigned equivalent | Resize text region without stealing selection or sending a message. |
| Pipeline background pan | Required | Pointer capture continues outside graph until release/cancel; arrow keys pan without activating a node. |
| Canvas agent coordinates | Intentionally retired | D-077: ownership ordering replaces spatial positions; no persisted coordinate migration is claimed. |
| Dock-zone drag, tab insertion, split resize | Intentionally retired | D-077: fixed workspaces and explicit regional separators replace arbitrary docking. |
| Floating panel move, eight-edge resize, z-order | Intentionally retired | D-077: whole-workspace native windows replace separate in-app floating panels, with no arbitrary multi-panel composition. |
| Whole-workspace native bounds | Required | Resize/move detached window, reopen it and verify restored bounds on each supported platform. |
| Detached close/reattach ownership | Required | Source reclaims terminal only after native ownership returns; browser does not invoke native-only operations. |

## Existing-task evidence and activity checkpoint — 2026-09-22

- P-160 now shares the structured editor with creation. Add/edit/remove remains in the task draft until acknowledged Save. All artifact fields are editable, with uploaded storage paths protected; external reference paths remain editable. Tab switches preserve unfinished artifact drafts. Save remains disabled until the artifact edit is saved or cancelled. Sparse evidence merging preserves unrelated concurrent additions and unchanged metadata from the latest known task detail; it does not provide server conflict detection.
- Cancel preserves original evidence, cleans only new uploads and retains failed cleanup for retry. Removing one reference does not delete a file still referenced by saved evidence. File deletion follows successful persistence, and retry does not repeat the acknowledged edit. ## Worktree mutation acknowledgement checkpoint — 2026-09-22

- Inspector checkpoint, rollback, rebase, PR, merge and removal now use explicit keyed HTTP writes with a duplicate-pending guard. Confirmations and merge drafts remain after refusal; rollback/removal dismiss only after the requested target/result is confirmed. A lost/malformed/mismatched acknowledgement keeps the exact retry payload/key, has a bounded 120-second wait, and never replays automatically on reconnect. A known refusal starts a fresh key on the next explicit attempt.
- Accepted results refresh scoped reads; cached terminal merge progress cannot leave controls disabled. Pending PR links, merge errors and cleanup warnings remain visible. The submitted target survives an agent-closing merge long enough to show its result without granting further writes against an unavailable agent. D-145 records the contract and retry limitations.
- Explicit checkpoint now distinguishes a clean no-op from Git failure; automatic checkpoint callers retain their best-effort default. Rollback checks the actual manager result, and PR responses include the target/outcome. Missing removal targets return an explicit refusal. The HTTP coordinator is restricted to the six keyed worktree commands, joins overlapping retries even after a waiter disconnects, rejects a changed payload sharing a pending key, and preserves the existing completed-response cache and unrelated command paths. No crash/partial-side-effect atomicity is claimed.
- The browser rebase case exposed a pre-existing P-243 defect: valid blocked preflight data carrying `error` was discarded as a failed read, hiding Rebase. Shape-validated preflight refusals are now retained as domain data, exposing stale/conflict recovery and continuing to block PR/merge. This has a focused component regression and actual Git base-advance acceptance.

Earlier test-only lint/type errors were corrected; existing bundle-size warnings remain.

### 2026-09-23 — Settings navigation preserves drafts and pending saves

- P-253 routes section, workspace, group, command-palette, same-window Classic-link and native-menu navigation through a shared owner guard. The complete action waits for explicit discard; cancellation keeps the mounted form, editor focus/caret, values and local secret buffers without sending the requested navigation or any settings write. Discard clears unsaved state, including secret-entry buffers, and performs the requested action once. An unchanged or reverted draft leaves without a prompt.
- Pending saves keep Settings mounted and preserve the original scope and requested destination. The operator sees the result before deciding whether to leave; failed saves retain the draft, successful completion does not trigger automatic navigation, and already saved scopes are not rolled back by discarding remaining edits. The existing coordinated save transport is unchanged.
- Active-group changes from the daemon cannot silently replace an edited/saving form. A visible retained-group notice offers an explicit switch; the real browser run saved to the original group after another group became active and proved the other group's settings were untouched. External detached-window ownership changes also preserve the local form. Group context-menu Settings now uses the selected row's group.
- Focused App regressions grew from **71 to 77 tests**. They cover section/workspace cancellation, keyboard exit, exact-once group navigation, pending/refused/successful save transitions, native-menu and command-palette cancellation, discarded secrets, clean exits, external group changes and native detach gating/ownership. ### 2026-09-23 — Search and reveal settings without replacing drafts

- P-254 adds local search over mounted labels, associated descriptions and section/disclosure scopes, including closed controls and appearance toggle buttons. It never indexes draft values, option text or secret inputs. Results show their scopes, announce counts/empty state, mount 20 initially and expose Show more. Enter opens the first available result without submitting; Arrow Down enters results; Escape/Clear resets only search.
- Result activation opens ancestor disclosures and focuses/scrolls the retained control. Search never filters away or remounts the form. A scoped MutationObserver refreshes results for changed controls and disabled states, ignores its own results and preserves the query/caret. D-151 records the UX.

### Settings dirty state and appearance lifecycle checkpoint — 2026-09-25

- P-292/P-293 are implemented under D-172. Caption, Save availability and navigation protection now share the actual pending-change predicate. Ordinary fields reverted to the baseline are clean and a clean submit sends no write; staged resets, explicit Relay overrides and AI clear intents retain their pending semantics.
- Settings owns appearance drafts. Every appearance control previews document styles without persistence; Reset stages defaults and accent buttons cannot submit the form. Appearance-only edits are guarded. Save commits local appearance after coordinated daemon acknowledgements; refusal/confirmation retains the preview, and local-storage failure reports an error with manual retry without replaying acknowledged server changes. Unmount/discard restores the persisted appearance; save/reload establishes the new baseline.
- Verification: focused lifecycle/AI safety/preferences checks passed **20 tests in three files**. `make ui-check` passed **85 files / 663 tests**, lint, typecheck, build and asset verification. Final fixture-only locator edits passed a separate full UI lint. ### Live terminal appearance and scrollback checkpoint — 2026-09-25

- P-294/P-295 are implemented under D-173. New terminals use current document font/background/text/accent/selection tokens and normalized saved scrollback. Existing xterm instances observe appearance changes and consume saved global settings without remounting the controller or socket. Color-only changes avoid a fit; font changes use the established reading/tail-preserving fit and visible-owner geometry gate. Disposal disconnects the observer.
- Scrollback follows Classic's 100–100,000 bounds, flooring and 2,000-line fallback; the Settings fallback is aligned with the daemon's 2,000 default. Changes arrive from projection deltas, acknowledged settings reads and reconnect snapshots. Local unsaved settings drafts never change terminal history. Reducing the limit intentionally trims older history; surviving reading context remains in place.
